Lean by issue

From the bills this legislator authored or co-authored. Each bill’s direction is measured against current law; lead authors count 3×, principal co-authors 2×, co-authors 1×. A tag needs at least 3 directional bills; Restrictive is a weighted average of −0.25 or lower, Permissive is +0.25 or higher, otherwise Balanced. The dark tick is the average of all Massachusetts bills on the topic. Click a topic to see the bills behind it.

Lean by issue

From the bills this legislator authored or co-authored. Each bill’s direction is measured against current law; lead authors count 3×, principal co-authors 2×, co-authors 1×. A tag needs at least 3 directional bills; Restrictive is a weighted average of −0.25 or lower, Permissive is +0.25 or higher, otherwise Balanced. The dark tick is the average of all Massachusetts bills on the topic. Click a topic to see the bills behind it.

These come from what this legislator authored and how they voted on tagged bills, with the bills linked below. A co-author may sign on to a bill they have reservations about, and a vote reflects one amendment stage. Most bills that reach a floor vote pass with large majorities and almost every member votes aye, so aye counts mostly mirror what reached the floor; authorship is the more distinctive signal. Treat the tags as a summary of the record, not a rating. Only floor votes are counted; committee votes are not matched to members yet.
